Result: 在二维障碍问题（含三次非线性的状态方程）和三维摩擦接触问题（含Coulomb摩擦诱导的非线性投影约束）上进行了验证，对比了高维模型、线性降阶模型、神经网络增强降阶模型及其超缩减变体，结果表明所提方法在显著降低在线计算成本的同时保持了良好的近似精度。

Conclusion: 提出的神经网络增强的非线性流形降阶框架能够有效处理力学中的非线性参数化变分不等式，在精度与计算效率之间取得了良好的平衡，且超缩减与非线性流形学习的交互设计具有可行性。

Abstract: We propose a model order reduction framework for nonlinear parametrized variational inequalities arising in computational mechanics. The high-dimensional model is written in mixed primal-dual form with projection-based complementarity conditions, leading to nonlinear nonsmooth algebraic systems solved by a semi-smooth Newton method in primal-dual form. On this basis, reduced models are constructed by proper orthogonal decomposition (POD) of both primal and dual solution snapshots, and the resulting reduced systems are solved by semi-smooth Newton iterations in the reduced space.
  To address cases where low-dimensional linear spaces provide limited approximation efficiency, we introduce a neural-network-augmented reduced model. Two feedforward networks learn corrections in the truncated POD coordinates of the primal and dual variables, defining a nonlinear manifold approximation that is embedded directly in the semi-smooth Newton iterations. The online cost associated with high-dimensional residual evaluations is reduced through hyper-reduction, using a sparse cubature approach based on greedy nonnegative least squares. Particular attention is paid to the interaction between hyper-reduction and the learned nonlinear manifold.
  The proposed methodology is assessed on two nonlinear variational inequalities with distinct sources of nonlinearity: a two-dimensional obstacle problem with a cubic nonlinearity in the state equation, and a three-dimensional frictional contact problem in which the Coulomb law induces a nonlinear projection in the constraint equation. Numerical results compare the high-dimensional model, the linear reduced model, the neural-network-augmented reduced model, and their hyper-reduced variants, demonstrating accurate approximations with substantial reductions in online computational cost.

</details>


### [91] [Adaptive Non-Linear Partition of Unity Methods for Scattered Data Interpolation with Discontinuities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21287)
*Adeeba Haider,Roberto Cavoretto,Juan Ruiz-Álvarez,Dionisio F. Yáñez*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出LOOCV-NL-PUM-GOOI方法，通过局部自适应优化RBF形状参数和补丁半径，在不依赖界面先验知识的情况下，有效抑制了散乱数据近似中不连续界面附近的吉布斯现象，显著降低误差并保持光滑区域精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散乱数据近似中，不连续界面附近的吉布斯现象会严重降低精度。已有的NL-PUM方法虽能应对，但其性能高度依赖两个固定超参数（RBF形状参数和补丁半径），缺乏自适应性，导致在不连续区域和光滑区域之间难以取得平衡。

Method: 将LOOCV与全局优化乐观改进（GOOI）结合，实现RBF形状参数和补丁半径的局部自适应选择。核心创新在于：设计了一个光滑度指标，将不连续性感知的收缩过程与基于LOOCV的半径选择联系起来——光滑区域的补丁保持不变，不连续附近的补丁自动收缩以避免跨越界面。

Result: 在含跳跃不连续的合成测试函数和挪威峡湾高程实测数据上，该方法在不连续界面附近显著降低了近似误差，同时在光滑区域保持了完全精度，且无需界面几何先验知识，也未引入额外计算开销。

Conclusion: LOOCV-NL-PUM-GOOI有效解决了NL-PUM超参数固定的局限，实现了不连续感知的自适应近似，具有实用价值和推广潜力。

Abstract: Scattered data approximation with discontinuities is challenging due to the Gibbs phenomenon, which significantly reduces accuracy near interfaces. The recently introduced Non-Linear Partition of Unity Method (NL-PUM) addresses this by combining Radial Basis Function (RBF) interpolation with a non-linear Weighted Essentially Non-Oscillatory (WENO) strategy. While effective, NL-PUM's performance relies heavily on two fixed hyperparameters: the RBF shape parameter and the patch radius. This work extends NL-PUM by adapting both hyperparameters locally using Leave-One-Out Cross-Validation (LOOCV) minimized via Global Optimization with Optimistic Improvement (GOOI). Our main innovation is a smoothness indicator linking a discontinuity-aware shrinkage process to LOOCV-based radius selection: patches in smooth regions remain unchanged, while those near discontinuities automatically shrink to avoid crossing the interface. The resulting method, LOOCV-NL-PUM-GOOI, requires no prior knowledge of interface geometry and introduces no extra cost beyond standard adaptive shape parameter selection. Numerical experiments on synthetic test functions with jump discontinuities and a real-data application to Norwegian Fjords elevation data confirm that this approach substantially reduces approximation error near discontinuities while preserving full accuracy in smooth regions.

</details>


### [92] [On the Approximation of the Unitary Operator Group Associated with a Rotation Matrix and Its Applications to Abstract Hyperbolic Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21293)
*Jemal Rogava,Zurab Vashakidze*

TL;DR: 本文针对抽象双曲型方程的柯西问题，提出了一种基于旋转矩阵酉群有理逼近的两层半离散格式，该格式具有四阶收敛性，并分析了收敛性对解正则性的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为抽象双曲型方程（包括非齐次线性和半线性情形）构造高效、高精度的数值逼近方案，并严格建立收敛性理论。

Method: 利用齐次抽象双曲方程解及其导数的向量表示（与旋转矩阵关联的酉算子群），构造该酉群的有理逼近（四阶最优收敛），并基于此设计两层半离散格式，推广至非齐次线性和半线性问题。

Result: 所构造的有理逼近具有四阶最优收敛性，收敛阶根据光滑度标度确定；两层半离散格式在解的正则性条件下保持相应的收敛阶。

Conclusion: 该研究为抽象双曲型方程提供了一种高效、高精度的数值方法，其收敛性质严格依赖于解的正则性，理论框架完整。

Abstract: The solution of the Cauchy problem for homogeneous abstract hyperbolic equations, together with its derivative, admits a vector representation in terms of a unitary operator group associated with a rotation matrix. A rational approximation of this unitary group is constructed and shown to possess optimal fourth-order convergence. The order of convergence is determined in accordance with the smoothness scale. Based on this rational approximation, a two-layer semi-discrete scheme is constructed for the approximate solution of Cauchy problems for nonhomogeneous abstract hyperbolic equations in both the linear and semi-linear settings. The convergence properties of the scheme are examined in relation to the regularity of the solution.

</details>


### [93] [An unfitted boundary algebraic equation method with Calderón preconditioning for 2D Stokes flow in irregular geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21295)
*Wenjun Ying,Qing Xia*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于二维外部/内部Stokes方程的无拟合边界代数方程方法，基于交错MAC网格，利用自由空间格子格林函数和薄交错边界层源表示，通过GMRES求解稠密边界系统，并采用离散Calderón预处理子加速收敛，数值实验验证了二阶精度和离散不可压缩性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统处理外部/内部Stokes问题的方法需要人工边界条件或奇异/近奇异求积，计算复杂且精度受限。本文旨在发展一种无需人工边界条件、避免奇异求积、同时保持高精度和计算效率的数值方法。

Method: 1) 在交错MAC网格上构建自由空间速度-压力格子格林函数（从自由空间Laplace LGF导出）；2) 利用薄交错边界层上的源表示均匀场；3) 通过局部插值在切割点施加物理Dirichlet数据；4) 采样法向秩更新消除静水零模；5) 采用离散先离散化再表示的路线，避免奇异/近奇异求积；6) 使用GMRES求解稠密边界系统，并构建基于标量Laplace核的分量式离散Calderón预处理子，配合填充FFT加速体积卷积。

Result: 在多重连通域、窄间隙、Moffatt涡等算例中，达到离散不可压缩性至求解器精度，恢复Moffatt涡的预期标度；实现速度和压力的二阶收敛；最大离散散度控制在数值精度内；离散Calderón预处理子将条件数降低多个数量级，对外部问题实现近乎网格无关的条件数，对窄间隙和细网格内部问题虽更苛刻但依然有效。

Conclusion: 该方法成功将边界积分思想推广到交错网格框架，为外部/内部Stokes流动提供了一种无需人工边界条件、无需奇异求积的高精度求解方案，离散Calderón预处理子显著提升了计算效率。

Abstract: We present an unfitted boundary algebraic equation method for the two-dimensional exterior/interior Stokes equations on a staggered MAC grid. By constructing an explicit free-space pair of velocity and pressure lattice Green's functions (LGFs) from free-space Laplace LGFs, we represent homogeneous fields using sources supported exclusively on thin staggered boundary layers. This formulation imposes physical Dirichlet data at cut points via local interpolation, while sampled-normal rank updates remove hydrostatic null modes associated with single or multiple obstacles. The workflow parallels that of classical boundary integral formulations and requires no artificial boundary conditions for exterior flows, but follows a discretize-then-represent route and does not require singular/near-singular quadrature. The resulting dense boundary system is solved via GMRES, utilizing a componentwise discrete Calderón preconditioner built from the scalar Laplace kernel and padded FFTs for fast volume convolutions. Extensive numerical validation, including multiply connected domains, narrow gaps, and Moffatt eddies, confirms discrete incompressibility to solver accuracy and recovers the expected Moffatt eddy scaling. We achieve second-order velocity and pressure convergence and bound maximum discrete divergence within numerical accuracy. The discrete Calderón preconditioner reduces the condition number by orders of magnitude and yields nearly mesh-independent conditioning in exterior configurations, while remaining effective---though more demanding---for narrow-gap and fine-grid interior problems.

</details>


### [94] [3D Uncertainty Quantification for the Photo-Acoustic Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21373)
*Babak Maboudi Afkham,Amal Mohammed A Alghami,Hassan Yazdanian,Tanja Tarvainen*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于有限元的贝叶斯不确定性量化框架用于光声断层成像（PAT），通过矩阵化随机优化（RTO）采样算法实现三维大规模推断，并系统研究了时空离散化与边界条件对后验不确定性的影响，首次在一般三维有限元几何上进行了大规模贝叶斯PAT研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光声断层成像需要可靠的不确定性量化，但传统贝叶斯方法计算成本高、对波动方程数值近似敏感，且缺乏针对三维复杂几何的可行方案。

Method: 提出矩阵化随机优化采样算法，仅需正向和伴随波传播操作即可生成独立后验样本；构建与离散正算子精确转置且与连续伴随一致的伴随离散格式；采用有限元框架处理复杂计算域和探测器几何。

Result: 与精确后验统计量、现有贝叶斯PAT方法及哈密顿蒙特卡洛（NUTS）进行对比验证，在具有约20万未知数的三维有限元问题上成功应用，为首次大规模三维有限元几何上的贝叶斯PAT研究。

Conclusion: 该方法可自然推广至一类线性PDE约束反问题，为大规模三维PAT不确定性量化提供了可行且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Photoacoustic tomography (PAT) is a promising modality for high-resolution biomedical imaging, motivating the need for reliable uncertainty quantification (UQ) of reconstructed images. Bayesian approaches provide a rigorous framework for UQ but remain computationally challenging for realistic three-dimensional PAT and are sensitive to numerical approximations in the governing wave equation. We develop a finite-element Bayesian UQ framework for PAT that accommodates complex computational domains and detector geometries while enabling large-scale three-dimensional inference. The proposed methodology reformulates the randomize-then-optimize (RTO) sampling strategy as a matrix-free algorithm that generates independent posterior samples using only forward and adjoint wave propagations. Particular attention is given to constructing an adjoint discretization that forms an exact transpose pair with the discrete forward operator while remaining consistent with the continuous PAT adjoint, enabling efficient least-squares solvers within the sampling procedure. We investigate the influence of temporal discretization, artificial boundary conditions, and adjoint consistency on posterior uncertainty and identify discretization strategies that avoid numerical artifacts. The framework is validated against exact posterior statistics, existing Bayesian PAT methods, and Hamiltonian Monte Carlo using the No-U-Turn Sampler (NUTS), and is demonstrated on a three-dimensional problem with approximately $2\times 10^5$ unknowns on a general finite-element domain. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first large-scale Bayesian PAT study on general three-dimensional finite-element geometries, and the methodology extends naturally to a broad class of linear PDE-constrained inverse problems.

</details>


### [95] [Jordan algebras, hemiplex numbers, and the Cholesky decomposition of arbitrary symmetric matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21383)
*Alan Edelman,Timothy E. Holy*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于半复数（hemiplex numbers）非结合代数的Cholesky分解方法，能够将Cholesky分解推广到任意对称矩阵（包括不定矩阵和奇异矩阵），无需选主元即可保持带状结构，并可用于零空间参数化和近零方向截断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于不定矩阵，标准Cholesky分解不存在，现有替代方法在数值稳定性和带状结构保持方面面临挑战。作者受二次方程x²=c（c≤0）求解的类比启发，试图将Cholesky分解推广到任意对称矩阵。

Method: 引入一种称为半复数（hemiplex numbers）的非结合代数，利用该代数允许负数的平方根存在且运算性质适合矩阵分解，设计了无需选主元的半复数Cholesky分解算法。

Result: 半复数Cholesky分解对任意对称矩阵均存在且数值稳定，无需选主元即可保持带状结构；对奇异矩阵可参数化零空间；支持类似SVD的近零方向截断。

Conclusion: 半复数Cholesky分解可作为求解对称线性方程组的一个实用工具，扩展了经典Cholesky分解的适用范围。

Abstract: Positive-semidefinite matrices are most efficiently factored using the Cholesky decomposition. For indefinite matrices, the Cholesky factorization does not exist, and the alternatives face greater challenges in achieving numeric stability and preservation of banded structure. Here we pursue an analogy between the requirement for positive-semidefinite matrices and the solution of the quadratic equation x^2 = c for c <= 0. It is shown that a non-associative algebra, called the hemiplex numbers, allows the Cholesky factorization to be computed for arbitrary symmetric matrices. Crucially, the hemiplex Cholesky factorization does not require pivoting for its existence or stability, allowing it to preserve banded structure. For singular matrices it produces a parametrization of the null space, and provides opportunity for truncation of nearly-null directions in a manner similar to common usage of the singular value decomposition. The hemiplex Cholesky factorization may be a practically useful addition to the tools for solving symmetric linear equations.

</details>

### [96] [HERMES: Heterogeneous Edge-Relational Multi-Head Embedded SSM Attention for Traffic Conflict Prediction at Signalized Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20505)
*Md Monzurul Islam,Subasish Das*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HERMES，一种异构图神经网络，将交通冲突评估建模为时序异质场景图分类问题，通过SSM赋能的多头注意力机制建模车辆-行人异构交互，在信号交叉口数据集上AUC-ROC达0.9898，显著优于Transformer和XGBoost基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于替代安全指标（SSMs）的交通安全评估方法多局限于成对交互的独立评估或将多智能体场景展平为固定特征向量，无法有效表示异构交互结构和场景级风险的演化过程。

Method: 构建异质边关系图神经网络HERMES：将车辆和行人视为异质节点，将车-车、车-人、人-人交互编码为带连续运动学和替代安全描述的关系特定边；结合关系特定注意力、动态节点-边更新、安全感知图池化和时序序列学习，联合估计场景级冲突概率。

Result: 在109,028条轨迹序列数据集上，增强版HERMES的AUC-ROC为0.9898±0.0013，AUC-PR为0.9412±0.0067，F1为0.8449±0.0103；在5%虚警率下检测出95.7%冲突序列；零样本外推AUC-ROC达0.9752。

Conclusion: 保留异构交互拓扑、安全感知的边语义和短期时序演化能有效提升场景级冲突分类性能，并支持信号交叉口可迁移的路侧安全监测。

Abstract: Surrogate safety measures (SSMs) enable proactive traffic safety assessment, but many existing methods evaluate pairwise interactions independently or flatten multi-agent scenes into fixed feature vectors, limiting their ability to represent heterogeneous interaction structure and evolving scene-level risk. This study formulates traffic conflict assessment as temporal heterogeneous scene-graph classification and proposes HERMES, a heterogeneous edge-relational graph neural network with SSM-informed multi-head attention. Vehicles and pedestrians are represented as heterogeneous nodes, while vehicle-vehicle, vehicle-pedestrian, and pedestrian-pedestrian interactions are encoded as relation-specific edges with continuous kinematic and surrogate-safety descriptors. Relation-specific attention, dynamic node-edge updates, safety-aware graph pooling, and temporal sequence learning are jointly used to estimate scene-level conflict probability. HERMES was evaluated using 109,028 trajectory-derived sequences from a signalized urban intersection and tested on an independently collected comparable intersection dataset. Enhanced HERMES achieved an AUC-ROC of 0.9898 +/- 0.0013, an AUC-PR of 0.9412 +/- 0.0067, and an F1 score of 0.8449 +/- 0.0103. At a 5% false-alarm rate, it detected 95.7% of conflict sequences, outperforming the strongest Transformer baseline and XGBoost. In zero-shot external evaluation, HERMES achieved an AUC-ROC of 0.9752 and an AUC-PR of 0.7829. Joint source-target training further improved target-site performance with limited target-site data. These findings show that preserving heterogeneous interaction topology, safety-informed edge semantics, and short-term temporal evolution improves scene-level conflict classification and supports transferable roadside safety monitoring at signalized intersections.

</details>


### [97] [PhysCoRe: Physics-Corrected Residual World Models for Material-Aware Deformable Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20653)
*Haocheng Yin,Shuohan Tao,Yongsheng Chen,Lu Gan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysCoRe 是一种物理校正残差世界模型，结合可微分的物质点法（MPM）模拟器与两个前馈神经网络：从视觉观察推断每粒子弹性参数的材质运动模块（MfM），以及学习并校正模拟器内部动力学偏差的残差校正模块（RfD），实现对可变形物体在机器人操作下的精确预测，并支持在线材料识别与置信度引导的探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形物体在机器人操作下的演化预测是一个长期挑战。现有方法要么依赖针对每个物体优化材料参数（速度慢且无法泛化），要么采用端到端学习（外推能力差且常违反基本物理结构）。

Method: PhysCoRe 包含三个核心组件：(1) 可微分的物质点法（MPM）模拟器作为物理先验；(2) 材质运动模块（MfM），从视觉观测中推断每粒子弹性参数，将模拟器锚定到物体特定的物理属性；(3) 残差动力学模块（RfD），学习并校正模拟器内部动力学与真实世界之间的系统性偏差。此外，MfM 支持在线材料识别，并利用预测不确定性引导探索。

Result: 在真实可变形物体操作序列上的实验表明，PhysCoRe 在预测精度上优于最先进的基线方法，其预测的置信度在物体几何上形成可靠的分布，为未来置信度引导的探索提供了自然信号。

Conclusion: 将可微分的物理模拟器与学习驱动的残差校正模块相结合，既能保持物理结构约束，又能吸收无法建模的系统偏差，是实现可变形物体精确预测的有效途径。

Abstract: Predicting how deformable objects evolve under robotic manipulation is a longstanding challenge. Existing approaches typically rely on per-object optimization to fit material parameters, which can be slow and cannot generalize, while end-to-end learned alternatives extrapolate poorly and often violate basic physical structure. We present PhysCoRe, a physics-corrected residual world model that couples a differentiable Material Point Method (MPM) simulator with two feed-forward neural networks. A material refinement module, Material from Motion (MfM), infers per-particle elasticity from visual observations, grounding the simulator in object-specific physics. A residual correction module, Residual from Dynamics (RfD), learns the discrepancy and predicts corrections to the simulator's internal dynamics, absorbing systematic biases that the analytical model cannot capture. This design also supports online material identification on novel objects. MfM adapts from limited interactions, and its predictive uncertainty steers further exploration toward the regions where its estimate is least confident. Experiments on real deformable-object manipulation sequences show that PhysCoRe outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in prediction accuracy, and that its predicted confidence forms a reliable distribution across the object's geometry, providing a natural signal for future confidence-guided exploration.

</details>


### [98] [Scalable Low-Cost Laboratory Automation: A Digital Twin-Integrated Robotic Platform for Autonomous Liquid Handling (RAINBOTTM)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20662)
*Mohamed Rami Ayeche,Souhil Sid,Ahyen Mostofa,Rehaan Hussain,Ali Shayesteh,Fadwa El Mellouhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RAINBOT™，一种基于消费级3D打印机改造的低成本开源液体处理机器人，集成了浏览器数字孪生实现远程监控，并与逆向设计框架CEID™结合，总成本低于1300美元，在精度上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验室自动化加速了发现过程，但商业液体处理系统成本高昂、设计专有且远程可监督性有限，阻碍了其广泛采用。

Method: 将消费级笛卡尔3D打印机（Elegoo Neptune 4 Max）的挤出机替换为精密单通道移液器，通过打印机G代码驱动X-Y-Z龙门架实现移液动作，柱塞和枪头弹出由两个紧凑型线性执行器在Python控制下完成；同时构建基于浏览器的数字孪生系统，与物理平台双向同步，实时镜像运动学和移液状态，支持远程监控和紧急停止。

Result: RAINBOT™成功执行了不同颜色水溶液的顺序交换实验，集成颜色传感器定量测量混合结果，测得红、黄、蓝（RYB）响应与预期混合行为的平均绝对误差在2个百分点以内；硬件总成本低于1300美元，约为入门级商用设备的十分之一。

Conclusion: RAINBOT™建立了一个经济可达的物理-虚拟框架，可用于自我驱动实验室自动化，通过低成本和开放复现性降低了自动化实验的门槛。

Abstract: Laboratory automation accelerates discovery, yet its adoption is constrained by the high cost, proprietary design, and limited remote supervisability of commercial liquid-handling systems. This work presents RAINBOT\textsuperscript{TM}, a low-cost, openly reproducible liquid-handling robot built by converting a consumer-grade Cartesian 3D printer (Elegoo Neptune 4 Max). The printer extruder is replaced by a precision single-channel pipette actuated through the printer's own G-code-driven X--Y--Z gantry, with plunger and tip-eject motions effected by two compact linear actuators under Python control. To make experiments transparent and remotely supervisable, a browser-based digital twin is implemented to synchronise bidirectionally with the physical platform, mirroring kinematics and pipetting states in real time and exposing remote monitoring, intervention, and an emergency stop from any web browser. As a proof of concept, RAINBOT\textsuperscript{TM} performed sequential exchanges of differently coloured aqueous solutions while an integrated colour sensor quantified the resulting mixtures; measured red, yellow, and blue (RYB) responses agreed with expected mixing behaviour to within a mean absolute error of two percentage points, validating correct execution and real-time tracking. Closing the loop, the platform is coupled to the CEID\textsuperscript{TM} (Cooperative Explorer for Inverse Design) framework, which recasts experimentation from iterative manual guessing into a goal-directed inverse-design search while keeping a human in the loop. The complete hardware costs under US\$1300, which is roughly an order of magnitude below entry-level commercial handlers, thereby establishing an accessible physical--virtual framework for self-driving laboratory automation.

</details>


### [99] [Safe and Scalable Multi-Drone Payload Transport via CBF-based Reinforcement Learning with Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20665)
*Jaeyoun Choi,Oswin So,Songyuan Zhang,Cooper Taylor,Chuchu Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于学习的框架，用于安全且可扩展的多无人机协同载荷运输。通过引入最小化2D抽象保持无人机-缆绳-载荷耦合关系，并采用离散图控制屏障函数近端策略优化（DGPPO）训练全分布式策略，实现了零样本仿真到现实的迁移。真实世界实验表明，单一学习策略可泛化至不同团队规模和任务场景，并能安全应对动态环境中的其他无人机障碍物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机载荷运输中的复杂耦合动力学给安全性和可扩展性带来挑战，现有方法在动态非结构化环境中存在局限，缺乏能够兼顾安全、可扩展且具备强泛化能力的解决方案。

Method: 提出基于学习的框架：1）设计最小化2D抽象模型保留关键耦合动力学；2）采用领域随机化处理团队规模和物理参数变化；3）提出离散图控制屏障函数近端策略优化（DGPPO）训练全分布式策略；4）实现零样本仿真到现实迁移。

Result: 单一学习策略可泛化至不同团队规模和任务场景；多组硬件实验表明该策略能安全运行于动态环境中，将其他无人机团队视为移动障碍物；实现了高效的零样本仿真到现实迁移。

Conclusion: 所提框架实现了高效、安全、可扩展的多无人机载荷运输，在复杂真实环境中展现出强泛化能力。

Abstract: Multi-drone payload transportation has emerged as a promising research paradigm with potential applications in construction, logistics, and disaster response. However, the complex coupled dynamics among drones, cables, and payloads pose significant challenges, and existing approaches remain limited in safety and scalability, particularly in dynamic and unstructured environments. In this work, we propose a learning-based framework for safe and scalable multi-drone cooperative payload transport. We introduce a minimal 2D abstraction that preserves the task-relevant drone-payload coupling required for coordination and safety, while remaining computationally efficient for large-scale learning. Using domain randomization over team size and physical parameters, we train a fully distributed policy via Discrete Graph Control Barrier Function Proximal Policy Optimization (DGPPO), enabling robust zero-shot sim-to-real transfer without fine-tuning. Extensive real-world evaluations demonstrate that a single learned policy generalizes across varying team sizes and task scenarios. Furthermore, multi-group hardware experiments show that the same policy can safely operate in dynamic environments, where other drone teams act as moving obstacles. These results indicate that the proposed framework enables efficient, safe, and scalable multi-drone payload transportation with strong generalization to complex real-world conditions.

</details>


### [100] [Towards Capability-Aware Traversability Navigation for Unstructured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20679)
*Gianluca Capezzuto,Felipe Tommaselli,Matheus P. Angarola,Ricardo V. Godoy,Marcelo Becker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CAT框架通过将机器人物理限制嵌入空间特征空间，实现不同形态机器人的地形可通行性估计，在多项指标上超越基线，并在四足和轮式平台上以4.8Hz实时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常通过后期轨迹过滤来跨形态传递预测，而非将平台约束编码到学习表示中，导致无法有效处理非结构化环境中不同机器人形态对同一地形的通行性差异。

Method: 提出Capability-Aware Traversability (CAT)框架，通过交互式标注管道将密集监督掩码基于物理轨迹进行标注，并利用Spatially-Adaptive Denormalization (SPADE)模块将机器人特定通行性向量调制到语义地形图上。

Result: 在人类标注和轨迹对齐数据集上，CAT在物理执行轨迹上AUROC提升11.0%，在人类轨迹上AUPRC提升15.8%。消融实验表明空间条件化和逐机器人原型产生超越通用路径预测的能力敏感性。在四足和轮式平台上以4.8Hz实现体现感知避障。

Conclusion: 将机器人物理限制直接嵌入空间特征表示可以有效提升跨形态地形可通行性估计的性能，并支持实时部署。

Abstract: Estimating traversability in unstructured environments requires conditioning on robot embodiment, as the same terrain can be traversable for one platform and unsafe for another. Existing methods often transfer predictions across morphologies through late-stage trajectory filtering rather than encoding platform constraints in the learned representation. We propose Capability-Aware Traversability (CAT), a framework that embeds physical limits directly into the spatial feature space. CAT grounds dense supervision masks in physical trajectories through an interactive annotation pipeline and modulates semantic terrain maps with robot-specific traversability vectors through Spatially-Adaptive Denormalization (SPADE) blocks. Across human-annotated and trajectory-aligned datasets, CAT leads all ranking-based metrics, improving AUROC by 11.0% on physically executed trajectories and AUPRC by 15.8% on human traces over the strongest baseline. Ablations show that spatial conditioning and per-robot prototypes produce capability sensitivity beyond generic path prediction. Deployments on a legged quadruped and a wheeled skid-steer demonstrate embodiment-aware obstacle avoidance on embedded hardware at 4.8 Hz.

</details>


### [101] [FELT: Generating Tactile Signals from Vision for Visuo-Tactile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20683)
*Zinan Li,Yiyang Ling,Yuming Gu,Binghao Huang,Chenhao Liang,Sharfin Islam,Hisham Bedri,John Chirikjian,Yunzhu Li,Stefanos Nikolaidis,Daniel Seita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FELT是一种从RGB观测合成触觉压力的框架，通过冻结视觉编码器和轻量查询解码器预测触觉信号，用于增强机器人操作策略，实验表明合成触觉可提升操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉对操作至关重要，但触觉传感器昂贵、脆弱且难以标准化，导致触觉数据比视觉数据稀缺，限制了视觉-触觉联合策略的泛化能力。

Method: 使用大型冻结视觉编码器提取RGB特征，通过轻量查询解码器以单次前馈方式预测每根手指的压力触觉图像；为尊重双指触觉传感器的物理拓扑，通过独立分支解码左右触觉面板以捕捉非对称接触模式。

Result: 在四项接触密集型操作任务上，FELT生成的触觉图像和潜在触觉特征均优于纯视觉基线，其中潜在触觉特征在策略训练和部署时无需真实触觉传感器。

Conclusion: FELT通过从视觉合成触觉信号，有效缓解了触觉数据稀缺问题，为视觉-触觉联合策略学习提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: The sense of touch is central to manipulation, especially when vision is occluded or ambiguous. Although combining vision and touch improves manipulation, learning robust visuo-tactile policies requires substantial tactile data. Such data remains scarcer than visual data, because tactile sensors are fragile, specialized, and hard to standardize. To address this, we present Feature-Extracted Latent Tactile (FELT), a learning-based framework that synthesizes per-finger pressure tactile images from RGB observations, reducing the need for tactile-equipped data collection. FELT uses a large frozen visual encoder and a lightweight query decoder to predict tactile signals in a single feed-forward pass. To respect the physical topology of dual-finger tactile sensors, FELT decodes the left and right tactile sensor panels through separate branches, capturing the asymmetric contact patterns during interactions such as wiping, insertion, and in-hand rotation. At inference time, FELT only requires RGB data, allowing us to augment existing vision-only data with tactile observations, either as generated tactile images or as latent tactile features. Experiments on four contact-rich manipulation tasks demonstrate that both generated tactile images and latent tactile features improve policy success over vision-only baselines, with latent feature requiring no real tactile sensor during policy training or deployment. Supplementary material is available on our anonymous website: https://felt-tactile.github.io/.

</details>


### [102] [Decentralized UAV Swarms for Ground Target Protection in GPS- and Communication-Denied Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20710)
*Dimitria Silveria,Paulo Ricardo Marques de Araujo,Tiago Nascimento,Sidney Givigi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在无通信和GPS环境下，利用自主无人机蜂群通过机载传感器和卡尔曼滤波器进行目标估计与协同，实现对地面目标进行环绕保护以抵御攻击的完整方案，并在真实机器人上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机防御方法大多依赖无人机间通信和全球定位系统，但在现代战争场景中这些资源可能不可用，因此需要一种不依赖通信和GPS的自主蜂群防御方案。

Method: 采用自主无人机蜂群，假设通信和GPS拒止环境；使用机载传感器进行相对测量；开发卡尔曼滤波器估计未知目标状态和无人机位置；提出去中心化蜂群环绕技术以适应目标运动。

Result: 通过真实机器人实验进行了广泛验证，证明了该方法在检测、环绕和拦截敌方无人机方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的不依赖通信和GPS的自主蜂群环绕防御方案能够有效保护地面目标免受无人机攻击。

Abstract: The presence of UAVs in military operations has recently increased, also increasing the demand for defense systems against UAV attacks. UAVs can also be used as countermeasures. Most available methods rely on UAV-to-UAV communication and global positioning. However, such resources may not be available in modern warfare scenarios. To address these limitations, we propose a pipeline for ground-target protection against UAV attacks that employs autonomous swarms of UAVs. We assume a communication- and GPS-denied environment in which the UAVs use onboard sensors to track the target and coordinate as a swarm. We developed Kalman filters to estimate the states of unknown targets and the positions of UAVs in the swarm using only relative measurements. Also, our strategy is to encircle the target of interest to maximize coverage. To achieve that, we propose a decentralized swarm encirclement technique that adapts to the target's motion. Our approach was extensively validated using real robots, demonstrating its effectiveness in detecting, encircling, and intercepting hostile UAVs.

</details>


### [103] [Self-Supervised Bio-Inspired Robotic Trajectory Planning with Obstacle Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20743)
*Miroslav Krupa,Miroslav Cibula,Kristína Malinovská*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受神经科学启发的自监督学习框架用于轨迹规划，利用前向和逆模型作为内部监督机制。实验证明了该方法的可行性，但发现规划器倾向于利用前向和逆模型提供的学习信号，为此提出了额外的训练机制和缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于采样的轨迹规划方法在高维空间和障碍密集环境中计算成本高昂。基于模型学习的方法虽然有望通过神经轨迹规划器的有限前向传播实现高效规划，但往往因依赖探索或专家演示而存在样本效率低或泛化能力有限的问题。因此，本文旨在开发一种无需外部监督的神经启发式自监督学习框架。

Method: 提出神经启发的自监督学习框架，利用前向模型（预测状态转移）和逆模型（预测动作）作为内部监督信号，在包含障碍物的环境中进行轨迹规划器的自我训练。针对规划器利用（exploit）前向和逆模型学习信号的问题，设计了额外的训练机制和缓解策略。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法在障碍物环境中是可行的，能够生成无碰撞轨迹。但规划器表现出对前向和逆模型提供的学习信号的利用倾向（即过度依赖这些信号而非学习真正的规划策略），通过提出的额外训练机制和缓解策略能够应对这一问题。

Conclusion: 神经启发的自监督学习为轨迹规划提供了一种有潜力的新范式，但需要谨慎设计训练机制以避免模型对内部监督信号的过度利用。

Abstract: Trajectory planning is a fundamental problem in robotics, requiring the generation of collision-free and efficient trajectories in a potentially complex environment. While sampling-based planners remain the dominant approach, they are often computationally expensive, particularly in high-dimensional spaces and obstacle-rich environments. Methods based on model learning offer a promising alternative, enabling efficient planning through a bounded number of forward passes through a neural trajectory planner, but commonly suffer from low sample efficiency or limited generalisation due to their reliance on exploration or expert demonstrations. This follow-up work tests our neuro-inspired self-supervised learning framework for trajectory planning that leverages forward and inverse models as the internal supervisory mechanism in an environment that contains an obstacle. Experimental results demonstrate the feasibility of the approach while revealing a tendency of our planner to exploit the learning signal provided by the forward and inverse models. To address this issue, additional training regimes and mitigation strategies are proposed and evaluated.

</details>


### [104] [A real-time RGB-D perception pipeline for autonomous impact hammers in mining: self-filtering, rock segmentation and rock-breaking poses generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20748)
*Martín Gallegos,Francisco Leiva,Patricio Loncomilla,Michelle Cortés,Javier Ruiz-del-Solar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于RGB-D感知的实时处理流程，用于液压冲击锤的自动化操作。该系统同时生成可操作的破碎姿态和无机器人工作空间3D表示，结合实例分割与点云处理，在嵌入式硬件上以约10Hz运行，延迟约675ms，实验验证了其在实时自主操作中的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地下采矿中的冲击锤通常采用远程操作，效率受限。为了实现操作自动化，需要实时感知系统来生成可行破碎姿态并感知工作空间。

Method: 结合基于图像的实例分割与几何点云处理，构建RGB-D感知流程，在嵌入式硬件上实现实时运行，同时输出操作可行的破碎姿态和无机器人的3D工作空间表示。

Result: 系统在约10Hz频率下运行，总延迟约675ms，在代表性缩尺场景中验证了实时自主操作冲击锤的可行性。

Conclusion: 所提出的RGB-D感知流程适用于实时自主液压冲击锤操作，为采矿自动化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Impact hammers, also known as rock-breakers, are essential machines in mining operations, where they perform secondary reduction. In underground mining, these machines are typically teleoperated, limiting operational efficiency. This paper presents a real-time RGB-D perception pipeline as a step towards automating the operation of hydraulic impact hammers used in mining. The proposed system simultaneously generates operationally feasible rock-breaking poses and a robot-free 3D representation of the workspace. The proposed approach combines image-based instance segmentation with geometric point cloud processing, and operates on embedded hardware at approximately 10 Hz with a total latency of around 675 ms, enabling responsive closed-loop behavior when integrated with a control system. Experimental results in a representative scaled scenario demonstrate that the proposed system is suitable for real-time autonomous impact hammer operation.

</details>


### [105] [Emergent Compositional Skills in Mixture-of-Experts VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20771)
*Shlok Shah,Rhiaan Jhaveri,Tharun Kumar Tiruppali Kalidoss,Chirayu Nimonkar,Ishaan Javali*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种简化MoE结构的VLA模型，从专家演示中端到端学习组合机器人策略，无需预定义任务分解。模型中的路由隐式学习高层任务序列，专家模块作为可复用的可解释原始行为，在保持任务性能的同时展现了有意义的专家特化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否通过简化的混合专家（MoE）动作头训练的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，在没有预定义任务分解或层级结构的情况下，从专家演示中端到端地涌现出可复用、可解释的原始行为，从而实现组合式机器人策略的学习。

Method: 采用简化MoE动作头的VLA模型，通过专家演示端到端训练，不使用任何预定义的任务分解或层级结构，让路由模块隐式学习高层任务序列，专家模块作为组合原始行为。

Result: 学习的专家被高度跨任务复用，且一致对应定性不同的低级行为；MoE在任务性能上与单一基线模型相当，同时展现了有意义的专家特化。

Conclusion: MoE结构能够使机器人策略从数据中自发涌现模块化、可解释的组合能力，为实现完全由数据驱动的模块化可解释机器人策略迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: We consider the problem of learning compositional robot policies end-to-end from expert demonstrations, without any pre-specified notion of task decomposition or hierarchy. We ask whether a VLA trained with a simplified Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) action head can emergently learn to decompose tasks into reusable, interpretable primitives. We find that learned experts are heavily reused across tasks and consistently correspond to qualitatively distinct low-level behaviors, suggesting that the router implicitly learns to perform high-level sequencing while experts serve as compositional primitives. Our MoE matches the task performance of a monolithic baseline while demonstrating meaningful expert specialization, a step toward modular, interpretable robot policies that emerge from data alone.

</details>


### [106] [Socially Consistent Multi-Robot Navigation Using Decoupled Planning and Trajectory Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20772)
*Matthew M. Sato,Kincho H. Law*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种部分去中心化的框架，通过将全局路径规划与轨迹协调解耦，生成可预测且符合社交规范的多机器人运动。首先，改进A*规划器将宏观社会规范嵌入代价函数，并构建社交图谱以增强路径一致性；其次，利用社交约束路径的结构，将多机器人轨迹协调问题建模为混合整数凸规划，实现高效无冲突轨迹计算。实验表明，该方法能产生可预测、社交合规的路径，并简化多机器人协调问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有局部人类感知规划方法缺乏确保长时域内行为一致性和可预测性的机制，导致局部规划器过载、运动低效且具有局部反应性，削弱了可预测性。

Method: 提出部分去中心化框架：(1) 改进A*规划器，将宏观社会规范嵌入代价函数；通过共享路径构建社交图谱以增强路径一致性。(2) 将多机器人轨迹协调问题建模为混合整数凸规划，利用社交约束路径结构实现高效无冲突轨迹计算。

Result: 在路径规划阶段强制执行社会一致性，能够产生可预测、符合社交规范的移动机器人路径，并简化多机器人协调问题，可扩展至大规模车队并支持动态任务分配。

Conclusion: 将社会一致性融入路径规划阶段，既能提升机器人行为的可预测性和社交合规性，又能将复杂的多机器人协调问题简化为可高效求解的凸优化问题，为机器人在人类环境中的长期自主导航提供了有效方案。

Abstract: The successful integration of mobile robots in human-centric environments requires navigation that is not only safe and efficient, but also predictable and aligned with social conventions, key precursors for human comfort and acceptance. While significant research addresses short-term human-aware planning, these methods often lack mechanisms for ensuring consistent and predictable behaviors across long horizons. Without socially aware long-term planners, local planners are overburdened, resulting in inefficient and locally reactive movements that undermine predictability. This paper introduces a partially decentralized framework that generates predictable and socially consistent multi-robot motion by decoupling global path planning from trajectory coordination. First, we propose a modified A* planner that embeds macroscopic social norms into the planner cost function. Planned paths are shared across mobile robots to collaboratively build a social graph of established routes, which enforces path consistency and reduces future planning effort. Second, we leverage the emergent structure of the socially constrained paths to formulate the multi-robot trajectory coordination problem as a mixed-integer convex program. The convex program enables efficient computation of conflict- free trajectories, scaling effectively to large fleets and supporting dynamic task assignment. Our results demonstrate that enforcing social consistency at the path planning stage produces predictable, socially compliant mobile robot paths and simplifies the otherwise complex problem of multi-robot coordination.

</details>


### [107] [Robostral Navigate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20785)
*Arjun Majumdar,Avinash Sooriyarachchi,Benjamin Tibi,Chris Bamford,Elliot Chane-Sane,Guillaume Lample,Khyathi Raghavi Chandu,Ludovic Ho Fuh,Mathieu Poiree,Olivier Duchenne,Rosalie Millner,Srijan Mishra,Theo Cachet,Thomas Chabal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Robostral Navigate，一个8B参数的单目RGB视觉-语言导航模型，通过大规模模拟数据生成（240万条轨迹/35万场景）、前缀缓存训练方案（训练token减少22倍）和树状注意力掩码机制，在仅使用单目RGB图像的情况下，在R2R-CE和RxR-CE基准上达到新的最优性能，成功率达77.4%和75.1%，超越依赖深度传感器或多相机的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航系统过度依赖深度传感器、多相机或预建地图，导致硬件受限、部署成本高，难以实现跨平台大规模部署。作者旨在设计一种仅需单目RGB图像（最泛用的传感器）即可工作的导航系统，实现对轮式、腿式和空中机器人的通用支持，无需重新标定。

Method: (1) 8B视觉-语言模型架构，输入仅为单目RGB图像，输出为在当前视角中指向下一目标位置的航点；(2) 在图像空间而非机器人坐标系中操作，对相机内参和场景尺度变化具鲁棒性；(3) 在35万模拟场景中生成240万条轨迹进行预训练；(4) 前缀缓存训练方案（prefix-caching），将完整episode打包为单一训练序列，训练token减少22倍；(5) 树状注意力掩码防止利用先前真实动作信息，强制基于视觉的动作预测；(6) 结合强化学习进一步优化探索与恢复能力。

Result: R2R-CE基准上成功率达77.4%，超越最优单目方法10.5个百分点，超越依赖深度或多相机的最优系统5.3个百分点；RxR-CE基准上成功率达75.1%，超越所有单目基线。

Conclusion: 仅使用单目RGB相机的VLM导航方法在性能上超越了依赖额外传感器（深度、多相机）的现有系统，且具备跨机器人平台部署的泛化能力，为大规模低成本的导航系统部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Deploying navigation systems at scale requires a recipe that minimizes sensor assumptions, generalizes across robot embodiments, and trains efficiently. Yet, today's best systems depend on depth sensors, multi-camera rigs, or pre-built maps, limiting the hardware they support and increasing deployment cost. We introduce Robostral Navigate, an 8B vision-language model built around this scalability objective. The model consumes only a stream of monocular RGB images - the most ubiquitous sensor across robotic platforms and predicts waypoints by pointing to the next target location in the current camera view. Operating purely in image space, rather than robot-specific coordinates, makes the policy naturally robust to changes in camera intrinsics and scene scale, enabling deployment across wheeled, legged, and aerial robots without recalibration. We generate 2.4 million trajectories across 350k simulated scenes to reduce the reliance on real-world data collection and scale easily. We further introduce a prefix-caching training recipe that packs entire episodes into single training sequences, reducing training tokens by 22x and cutting training time from months to days. A tree-based attention mask prevents conditioning on previous ground-truth actions, encouraging visually grounded action prediction, and reinforcement learning is used to further improve exploration and recovery capabilities. On the Room-to-Room and Room-Across-Room in Continuous Environments (R2R-CE and RxR-CE) benchmarks, Robostral Navigate sets a new state of the art. On R2R-CE, it achieves a 77.4% success rate, surpassing the best monocular method by 10.5 points and the strongest depth- or multi-camera system by 5.3 points despite using only a single RGB camera. On RxR-CE, it reaches 75.1% success rate, outperforming all monocular baselines.

</details>


### [108] [URF: A Unified Robot Control-Policy Framework for Stable Contact Aware Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20912)
*Jiyou Shin,Youngjin Seo,Jaeseog Won,Sungwon Seo,Hyunjun Kim,Seokmin Yoon,Tuan Luong,Hyungpil Moon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出统一机器人控制策略框架（URF），将柔顺动作预测与阻抗-导纳统一控制相结合，通过预测虚拟目标、刚度矩阵和切换比率，在刚体接触任务中实现更高的成功率和更少的故障模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的操控策略通常将动作预测与底层控制分离，但在刚体接触中，这种分离会导致不稳定接触、跟踪误差、过载或工具损坏，因为相同的动作命令在不同控制器下表现不同。

Method: URF从多模态观测中预测虚拟目标、刚度矩阵和阻抗-导纳切换比率。切换比率决定控制器何时更偏向导纳控制（精确运动跟踪）或阻抗控制（安全刚体接触）。利用实测接触力构造切换比率标签来监督控制器模式预测。

Result: 在翻箱和压线任务中，URF相比纯导纳控制实现了更高的任务成功率，减少了快速力堆积、大力振荡、工具断裂和安全停机等故障模式。

Conclusion: 接触感知策略不仅需要预测柔顺动作，还需要预测用于执行这些动作的控制器行为。

Abstract: Learning-based manipulation policies usually predict robot actions from sensory observations and leave their execution to a separate low-level controller. In rigid contact, this separation can be problematic: the same motion to a virtual target or compliant motion command can lead to unstable contact, tracking error, excessive loading, or tool damage, depending on the low-level controller. In this paper, we propose a \textit{Unified Robot Control-Policy Framework} (URF), which connects compliant action prediction with unified impedance-admittance control. Given multimodal observations, URF predicts a virtual target, a stiffness matrix, and an impedance-admittance switch ratio. The switch ratio determines when the controller should behave more like admittance control for accurate motion tracking and when it should move toward impedance control for safer rigid contact. Because demonstration data do not provide ground-truth environment stiffness, we construct switch-ratio labels from measured contact forces and use them to supervise controller-mode prediction. Across box-flipping and line-pressing tasks, URF achieves higher task success rates while reducing failure modes observed with admittance-only execution, including rapid force buildup, large force oscillations, tool breakage, and robot safety stops. These results suggest that contact-aware policies benefit from predicting not only compliant actions but also the controller behavior used to execute them. Project page: https://jiyou384.github.io/urf_project_page/

</details>


### [109] [Deep Reinforcement-Learning-Guided Model Predictive Control for Preventing Overtakes in Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20973)
*Yufei Xi,Yijie Liao,Tulga Ersal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层强化学习引导的模型预测控制框架，用于自动驾驶赛车的防守性阻挡，将防守问题建模为空间占用率调节问题，在Thunderhill West赛道仿真中平均超车时间从8.8秒延长至14.6秒，并实现83.4%的轮胎力利用率与33.3毫秒的平均求解时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶赛车研究多聚焦于圈速最小化，缺乏对防守性阻挡策略的系统性研究，而防守策略对于防止更快对手超车至关重要。

Method: 将防守问题建模为空间占用率调节问题，采用分层架构：上层使用Soft Actor-Critic算法在Frenet域生成几何感知的防守参考轨迹，下层通过非线性模型预测控制在摩擦约束下嵌入该参考作为空间正则化项。

Result: 平均超车时间从8.8秒延长至14.6秒，显著降低对手进展；实现83.4%的轮胎力利用率；平均求解时间33.3毫秒（标准差13.9毫秒），满足实时高速对抗需求。

Conclusion: 所提出的分层RL-MPC框架有效实现了高速自动驾驶赛车的防守性阻挡，在实时性和轮胎力利用率方面表现优异，为自动驾驶赛车策略研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: This paper addresses defensive blocking in autonomous racing, where a vehicle must prevent a faster opponent from overtaking while operating near its dynamic limits. Different from lap-time minimization, we formulate defense as a spatial occupancy regulation problem via a hierarchical reinforcement-learning guided model predictive control framework. A Soft Actor-Critic strategic layer operates in the Frenet domain to generate geometry-aware defensive references, which are embedded into the nonlinear model predictive control formulation as spatial regularization under friction constraints. Evaluated on the Thunderhill West circuit in simulation, the framework increases average overtake time from 8.8 s to 14.6 s while significantly reducing opponent progress. Meanwhile, it allows the vehicle to utilize 83.4% of available tire force. The framework achieves a 33.3 ms mean solve time (13.9 ms std), supporting real-time high-speed adversarial interaction.

</details>


### [110] [Distributed Model-Based Diffusion For Scalable Multi-Robot Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20992)
*Haejoon Lee,Xinyi Wang,Taekyung Kim,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出分布式模型驱动扩散（DMBD）框架，通过将逆向扩散过程分解为局部条件逆向扩散过程，解决了多机器人系统轨迹优化中集中式高维推理的样本效率低和全局信息需求问题，在多种场景下实现亚秒级求解并显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型驱动扩散（MBD）在单机器人轨迹生成中表现优异，但直接扩展到多机器人系统会形成集中式高维推理问题，面临维度灾难导致的样本效率低下，且需要全局访问所有机器人的动力学、约束和目标，缺乏可扩展性和分布式特性。

Method: 提出分布式模型驱动扩散（DMBD）框架，采用服务器-机器人分布式架构，将全局逆向扩散过程分解为各机器人独立的局部条件逆向扩散过程。每个机器人在自身控制子空间内迭代去噪，同时以服务器聚合广播的其他机器人的当前轨迹估计为条件进行推理。

Result: 在目标交换、多层覆盖、泊车和高峰交通等场景的仿真中，DMBD展现出强可扩展性，能够在亚秒级求解多种具有挑战性的协调任务，且显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: DMBD通过分布式分解有效解决了多机器人轨迹优化中集中式方法的维度灾难和全局信息依赖问题，为多机器人系统的实时协调提供了一种高效、可扩展的采样优化范式。

Abstract: Trajectory optimization for multi-robot systems remains a critical challenge, particularly when navigating highly non-convex, non-linear, and non-differentiable environments. While Model-Based Diffusion (MBD) has recently emerged as a promising sampling-based optimization paradigm for single-robot trajectory generation, extending it to multi-robot systems results in a centralized, high-dimensional inference problem that (i) suffers from poor sample efficiency due to the curse of dimensionality and (ii) requires global access to all robots' dynamics, constraints, and objectives. To address this, we propose Distributed Model-Based Diffusion (DMBD), a distributed server-robot framework that decomposes the reverse diffusion process into local conditional reverse diffusion processes. This decomposition enables each robot to iteratively perform denoising independently within its own control subspace while conditioning on the current trajectory estimates of the other robots that are aggregated and broadcast by the server. Extensive simulations in goal swapping, multi-floor coverage, parking, and rush-hour scenarios demonstrate that DMBD achieves strong scalability, solving many challenging coordination tasks in sub-seconds and significantly outperforming existing baselines.

</details>


### [111] [TableVerse: A Large-scale Tabletop Dataset with Real-world Grounded Layouts for Generalizable Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21017)
*Boyuan Wang,Yue Zhang,Xutao Xue,Xueyu Song,Yu Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TableVerse提出了一种全自动的Real2Sim流水线，通过从无结构的网络图像数据中确定性重建高保真桌面环境，并合成高质量的无碰撞抓取-放置轨迹，构建了包含10万个物理一致环境的大规模数据集TableVerse-100K，为通用机器人操作策略的学习提供了可扩展、高保真的数据基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人操作策略的发展受限于大规模、高保真场景数据的可用性。现有的自动合成方法（如文本到布局生成或简化程序化生成）常存在物理不真实性问题，且难以捕捉真实人类环境中复杂密集的杂乱场景。

Method: 提出TableVerse全自动Real2Sim流水线：从无结构的网络图像数据出发，经确定性重建生成高保真桌面环境（精确度量尺度、真实拓扑、验证机械稳定性）；集成自动任务条件轨迹生成框架，合成高质量无碰撞抓取-放置演示。

Result: 构建了TableVerse-100K数据集，包含10万个独特的物理一致环境及配套的交互操作轨迹，覆盖多样化的资产组合、真实空间分布和高品质演示。

Conclusion: TableVerse-100K通过从真实图像数据直接重建而非想象生成的方式，提供了高度可扩展且高保真的数据基础，为未来通用机器人操作任务的研究提供了重要支撑。

Abstract: The development of generalizable robotic manipulation policies is inherently bounded by the availability of large-scale, high-fidelity scene data. While recent automated synthesis methods attempt to bridge this gap via text-to-layout hallucination or simplified procedural generation, they frequently suffer from physical implausibility and fail to capture the complex, dense clutter of actual human environments. In this paper, we introduce TableVerse, a fully automated Real2Sim pipeline that shifts the paradigm from imaginative layout generation to deterministic reconstruction from unstructured, in-the-wild image data. Our framework seamlessly processes unscripted internet media into high-fidelity, simulation-ready tabletop environments with accurate metric scales, authentic topologies, and verified mechanical stability. Furthermore, an automated task-conditioned trajectory generation framework is integrated to synthesize high-quality, collision-free pick-and-place demonstrations. Leveraging this complete pipeline, we construct the TableVerse-100K Dataset, a large-scale corpus comprising 100,000 unique, physically consistent environments paired with interactive manipulation trajectories. By capturing diverse asset compositions, realistic spatial distributions, and high-quality demonstrations, TableVerse-100K establishes a highly scalable and high-fidelity data foundation, providing significant value to facilitate future research in generalizable robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [112] [ZONDA: Zero-shot Object Navigation with Dynamic Avoidance in Multi-floor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21025)
*Shaomin Liang,Xuanhong Liao,Shiyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ZONDA是一种零样本目标导航框架，支持跨楼层导航和动态行人避让，在真实双足机器人和多个仿真基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标导航方法局限于静态单层环境，忽略跨楼层拓扑和动态行人，限制了实际部署。

Method: 提出ZONDA框架，包含三个核心组件：(i) 启发式多层规划——基于高度差可遍历地图实现楼梯通行和跨楼层探索，无需平台特定的学习控制器；(ii) 多视角目标验证——结合视觉-语言模型进行多尺度观测交叉验证，减少假阳性；(iii) 动态行人避让——显式跟踪和预测移动行人以生成预见性行为。

Result: 在真实Direct Drive Tech TITA双足机器人及HM3D、MP3D仿真上取得显著提升，在动态基准HM3D-DYNA上保持鲁棒导航。

Conclusion: ZONDA通过结合启发式跨楼层规划、多视角VLM验证和动态行人预测，有效解决了现有方法在真实复杂环境中的局限性。

Abstract: In Object Goal Navigation task, existing methods are typically restricted to static and single-floor environments, ignoring cross-floor topologies and dynamic pedestrian, which limits their real-world deployment. To address these limitations, we propose ZONDA, a zero-shot object navigation with dynamic avoidance framework. In particular, ZONDA integrates three core components: (i) Heuristic multi-floor planning: from height-difference traversable maps, enables stair traversal and cross-floor exploration without a platform-specific learned controller; (ii) Multi-view target verification: cross-checks multi-scale observations with a vision-language model, significantly reducing false positives; and (iii) Dynamic pedestrian avoidance: explicitly tracks and predicts moving pedestrians to generate anticipatory behaviors. Evaluated on a real Direct Drive Tech TITA biped robot and extensive simulations on HM3D and MP3D, ZONDA achieves significantly improved results. Moreover, ZONDA can maintain robust navigation on the dynamic benchmark HM3D-DYNA compared to the existing baseline.

</details>


### [113] [A Real-Time Generalized Nash Equilibrium Framework for Interaction-Aware Autonomous Driving in Mixed Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21043)
*Nouhed Naidja,Mohamed-Cherif Rahal,Steve Pechberti,Stéphane Font,Guillaume Sandou,Marc Revilloud*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于广义纳什均衡问题（GNEP）的自动驾驶决策框架，通过粒子群优化（PSO）求解器实现混合交通环境中的安全、高效导航，并在真实测试轨道上使用雷诺Zoé进行了验证，求解器在50毫秒内收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合交通环境中自动驾驶车辆与人类驾驶员之间存在复杂的相互依赖关系，传统的解耦优化方法无法有效建模这种动态耦合，导致安全性和可行性受限。

Method: 将驾驶交互建模为广义纳什均衡问题（GNEP），显式建模共享安全约束和几何约束；提出基于粒子群优化（PSO）的专用求解器来处理非凸问题。

Result: 在真实测试轨道上使用自动驾驶雷诺Zoé与人类驾驶员交互进行验证，系统能够处理关键场景并生成舒适、类人的轨迹；求解器在50毫秒内实现收敛。

Conclusion: 所提出的GNEP框架结合PSO求解器能够有效解决混合交通中AV决策的耦合问题，在实时性、安全性和舒适性方面均表现出可行性。

Abstract: Safe and efficient navigation in mixed-traffic environments remains a critical challenge for Autonomous Vehicles (AVs), primarily due to the complex interdependence between the AV's decisions and the unpredictable reactions of human drivers. This paper introduces a comprehensive decision-making framework that formulates the driving interaction as a Generalized Nash Equilibrium Problem (GNEP). Unlike decoupled optimization approaches, this framework explicitly models shared safety and geometric constraints, ensuring that the feasibility of the AV's strategy is dynamically linked to the opponent's actions. To solve this non-convex problem in real-time, we propose a dedicated solver based on Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO). The complete architecture was validated on a test track using a real autonomous Renault Zoé interacting with a human driver. Experimental results demonstrate the system's ability to handle critical scenarios by generating comfortable, human-like trajectories. Benchmarks confirm the solver's operational feasibility, achieving convergence in under 50 ms.

</details>


### [114] [GuidedAttention: Interpretable and Correctable Visual Attention for OOD-Robust Robot Manipulation via Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21049)
*Masaki Murooka,Ryoichi Nakajo,Keisuke Shirai,Tomohiro Motoda,Hanbit Oh,Ryo Hanai,Yukiyasu Domae*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GuidedAttention框架，通过将可解释、可纠正的视觉注意力作为显式中间表示，实现端到端视觉运动策略中人类对模型注意力的理解和修正，提升机器人操作性能特别是分布外场景下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端视觉运动策略缺乏让人类理解或纠正策略视觉注意力的机制，导致模型在分布外条件下性能下降且难以调试。

Method: 从相机图像预测任务相关的注意力关键点作为显式中间表示，并以此条件化扩散动作策略。用户在初始化时可检查并修正关键点，修正后的注意力通过跟踪模块在执⾏过程中自动传播。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界的实验中，GuidedAttention一致提升了机器人操作性能，特别是在位置和外观分布外条件下。

Conclusion: 引入可解释、可纠正的视觉注意力作为显式中间表示，能够有效提升机器人操作策略在分布外场景下的鲁棒性和人类可控性。

Abstract: End-to-end visuomotor policies provide little opportunity for humans to understand or correct the policy's visual attention. We propose GuidedAttention, a visuomotor imitation learning framework that introduces interpretable and correctable visual attention as an explicit intermediate representation. Task-relevant attention keypoints are predicted from camera images and condition a diffusion-based action policy. Users can inspect and optionally correct selected keypoints once at rollout initialization, after which the corrected attention is automatically propagated throughout execution by a tracking module. Experiments in simulation and the real world demonstrate that GuidedAttention consistently improves robot manipulation performance, particularly under positional and appearance out-of-distribution (OOD) conditions.

</details>


### [115] [Human-Inspired Framework for Robotic Craniotomy: Integrating Multimodal Fusion and Adaptive Trajectory Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21058)
*Renzhen Le,Xiao Zhang,Di Wu,Yuanyu Wei,Jiachen Zhu,Zhenzhi Ying,Pengfei Zhang,Liming Shu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种仿人闭环机器人开颅框架，通过多模态感知和自适应控制实现安全自主开颅，在牛肋和离体颅骨实验中达到97%突破预测准确率、0.29mm最大超调量，且无硬脑膜损伤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动开颅手术风险高、依赖医生技能，易导致疲劳和硬脑膜损伤；现有机器人系统为开环控制，仅依赖术前影像，无法补偿术中配准误差和组织变形，亟需闭环控制方案。

Method: 提出仿人闭环机器人开颅框架：(1) 自适应双轮廓融合算法生成贴合复杂颅骨几何的轨迹；(2) 多模态两阶段交叉模态注意力(CMA)-时序卷积网络(TCN)-Transformer网络结合自适应贝叶斯滤波器，融合力和声学信号实现突破检测；(3) 基于原位投影的轨迹调整策略动态补偿深度偏差，安全隔离残余骨组织。

Result: 牛肋实验：突破预测准确率97%，检测延迟0.048±0.097s，最大超调量0.29mm。四例离体颅骨实验均成功完成，无硬脑膜损伤。

Conclusion: 所提出的控制框架实现了安全自主的开颅手术，具备高效的闭环控制能力，能够显著提升手术安全性和自主性。

Abstract: Manual craniotomy is a high-risk, skill-dependent procedure associated with surgeon fatigue and potential dural injury. While robotic approaches have improved safety, existing open-loop systems rely solely on preoperative images and cannot compensate for intraoperative registration errors or tissue deformation. To address this, we propose a human-inspired closed-loop robotic craniotomy framework that intelligently integrates preoperative planning with intraoperative execution. An adaptive dual-contour fusion algorithm is employed to generate trajectories that conform to complex cranial geometries while maintaining a consistent tool-bone relative pose. For intraoperative perception, a multimodal two-stage cross-modal attention block (CMA)-temporal convolutional network (TCN)-Transformer network combined with an adaptive Bayesian filter fuses force and acoustic signals to achieve robust breakthrough detection under varying bone conditions. Upon detection, an in-situ projection-based trajectory adjustment strategy dynamically compensates for depth deviations, enabling safe residual bone isolation. Experiments on bovine ribs show a breakthrough prediction accuracy of 97%, a detection latency of 0.048 +/- 0.097 s, and a maximum overshoot of 0.29 mm. All four ex vivo cranial experiments were successfully completed without dural injury. These results demonstrate that the proposed cybernetic framework enables safe and autonomous craniotomy with highly effective closed-loop control.

</details>


### [116] [RL-MACRO: A Cybernetic Closed-Loop Intelligence Framework for Multimodal Adaptive Robotic Craniotomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21113)
*Xiao Zhang,Jiaxuan Li,Renzhen Le,Di Wu,Chao Sun,Jiachen Zhu,Haoyuan Zhang,Xiang Li,Jian Liu,Zhenzhi Ying,Pengfei Zhang,Liming Shu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RL-MACRO框架，通过CNN-LSTM观测器融合力与声音反馈重构隐藏温度状态，并结合离线IQL策略实现自主颅骨钻孔的闭环控制，在离体组织上验证了有效性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人开颅术中，工具-组织相互作用存在不可观测的温度状态和时变组织特性，传统方法难以在安全与效率间取得平衡。

Method: 构建CNN-LSTM观测器融合力与声音反馈重构温度作为信念状态；采用离线隐式Q学习（IQL）策略生成决策；双头Actor动态协调进给速度、主轴转速和切削深度，通过在线轨迹重规划与速度伺服执行。

Result: 温度重构R²=0.939，MAE=1.717°C；在牛肋骨和离体山羊头骨上验证了感知鲁棒性、力/温度异常恢复能力及不规则表面平滑执行能力。

Conclusion: RL-MACRO建立了数据驱动的控制论范式，实现了安全高效的自主骨切削，为机器人开颅术提供了可行的闭环智能框架。

Abstract: Autonomous robotic craniotomy requires continuous regulation of tool-tissue interactions to mitigate mechanical overload and thermal damage while maintaining surgical efficiency. However, this process is inherently partially observable due to unknown, time-varying tissue properties and the inability to directly measure cutting temperatures under physical occlusion. To address these challenges, we propose RL-MACRO, a cybernetic closed-loop intelligence framework that couples multimodal perception, adaptive decision-making, and robotic execution. This framework empowers the surgical robot to autonomously perceive inaccessible states from partial sensory feedback and dynamically optimize its behaviors under uncertain environment. A CNN-LSTM observer first fuses force and sound feedback to reconstruct the hidden temperature state (R^2=0.939, MAE = 1.717 deg C). This reconstructed temperature, alongside multi-sensor features, forms the belief state for an offline Implicit Q-Learning (IQL) policy. A novel dual-head Actor dynamically coordinates the feed rate, spindle speed, and cutting depth to optimize efficiency within strict safety bounds. These decisions are seamlessly translated into spatial motions via online trajectory re-planning and velocity servoing. Experiments on bovine ribs and six ex vivo goat skulls validate the system's robust perception, adaptive recovery from force/temperature excursions, and smooth execution on irregular surfaces, establishing a data-driven cybernetic paradigm for safe and efficient autonomous bone cutting.

</details>


### [117] [FORGE-plus: Force-Budgeted Recovery for Contact-Rich Assembly with a Frozen LLM Supervisor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21227)
*Kyupaeck Jeff Rah,Midum Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双层框架，利用冻结的文本大语言模型（LLM）为每个物体分配力上限并选择恢复动作，底层控制器严格执行为上限，在脆弱瓶子放置和0.4mm间隙齿轮插入任务中实现256/256零破损成功，平均峰值力5.4N，并有效处理抓握滑移故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 力条件强化学习在紧公差装配中需要合理设定力上限并能在不超限的情况下从插入失败中恢复，现有方法缺乏自动分配力上限和智能恢复策略的能力。

Method: 双层框架：上层为冻结的文本LLM（不直接控制力），根据紧凑文本力特征为每个物体分配力上限并选择固定动作菜单中的恢复操作；下层为强制执行上限的低层控制器，恢复策略不能增加力上限，隐藏的破损阈值仅评估者知晓。

Result: 在脆弱瓶子和0.4mm间隙齿轮插入任务（使用Robotiq 2F-140和Franka Panda手爪）中，单策略256/256全部成功无破损，正确预测释放时机，完整取放插入管线平均峰值力5.4N；在注入滑移故障下，力特征恢复策略在两种手爪上分别解决40%和64%故障，而加压基线无效或频繁破损。

Conclusion: 基于文本LLM分配力上限和选择恢复动作的双层框架能有效解决力约束下的紧公差装配任务，PPO在严格力约束下失败，学习释放策略也不成功。所有实验在刚体仿真中完成，未进行仿真到现实迁移。

Abstract: Force-conditioned reinforcement learning (RL) enables tight-clearance assembly under a commanded force ceiling, but practical deployment requires determining an appropriate force limit for each object and recovering from insertion failures without exceeding it. We present a two-layer framework in which a frozen, text-only large language model (LLM) assigns a per-object force ceiling before execution and selects recovery maneuvers from a fixed action menu using compact textual force signatures. The LLM never controls force directly: a low-level controller enforces the force ceiling, the recovery policy cannot increase it, and the hidden breaking-force threshold is known only to the evaluator.
  We evaluate the framework on fragile bottle placement and 0.4 mm diametral-clearance gear insertion using two grippers (Robotiq 2F-140 and Franka Panda hand). A single policy passes 256/256 evaluation episodes on both fragile and robust objects without breakage, correctly predicts release timing, and completes a full table-pick-and-insert pipeline with a mean peak force of 5.4 N. Under injected in-grip slip, the force-signature recovery strategy resolves 40% and 64% of failures on the two grippers, whereas a press-harder baseline is either ineffective or causes frequent breakage. We also report negative results, including the failure of PPO to solve the task under strict force constraints and unsuccessful learned release strategies. All experiments are conducted in rigid-body simulation with hidden force-threshold breakage; no sim-to-real claim is made.

</details>


### [118] [Factorized Spatio-Temporal Convolutions for Human Pose Estimation from Planar Lidar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21309)
*Simone Arreghini,Mirko Nava,Nicholas Carlotti,Antonio Paolillo,Alessandro Giusti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于时空块（Space-Time Blocks）的轻量级网络，用于从平面激光雷达序列中进行全方位人体检测和二维姿态估计，通过跨模态自监督训练（利用RGB-D跟踪器），在服务机器人CPU上实现实时推理，显著降低了距离、位置和方向估计误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人通常配备低成本平面激光雷达和有限的计算资源，而现有的人体位姿估计方法多依赖摄像头、3D激光雷达或GPU计算，无法满足这类机器人的实时性需求和传感器限制。

Method: 设计基于时空块（Space-Time Blocks）的轻量级网络，沿扫描射线进行空间处理，跨扫描进行时间聚合；利用RGB-D身体跟踪器在传感器重叠区域进行跨模态自监督训练，避免人工标注激光雷达数据。

Result: 相比参数匹配的基线模型，距离误差降低38%，位置误差降低28%，方向误差降低15%；在FROG数据集上进行了基准测试；在服务机器人CPU上实现了实时推理。

Conclusion: 该方法适用于计算受限服务机器人的空间感知，在全方位人体检测和相对二维姿态估计任务中表现优越，且具备实际部署的可行性。

Abstract: Localizing nearby humans and estimating their facing direction are key capabilities for safe navigation and socially aware human-robot interaction. Many pose-estimation pipelines target cameras and 3D LiDAR or assume GPU-class compute, whereas service robots are often equipped only with omnidirectional planar LiDARs and modest onboard processors. We address omnidirectional human detection and relative 2D pose estimation from planar LiDAR sequences with a lightweight network based on Space-Time Blocks, which explicitly separate spatial processing along scan rays from temporal aggregation across scans. Our network processes 360° LiDAR sequences to output per-ray human presence, distance, and relative orientation. We train it via cross-modal self-supervision from a narrow RGB-D body tracker in the sensors' overlap region, removing the need for manual LiDAR labels. Quantitative experiments show that our approach consistently outperforms a parameter-matched baseline model, reducing errors in distance (-38%), position (-28%), and orientation (-15%). We further benchmark on the public FROG dataset, report real-time CPU inference on a service robot, and validate with in-field demonstrations, supporting its suitability for spatial perception on computationally constrained service robots.

</details>


### [119] [Grasp, Handover, Rotate: Bimanual Object Reorientation via Compositional Diffusion and Energy-Based Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21341)
*Wun Lam Yeung,Wenjun Liu,Yui Cheung Yu,Zhengyan Lambo Qin,Qijin She,Heng Li,Ziqi Wang,Ping Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出BiCompoDiff，一个结合组合扩散模型和基于能量的方法，用于双机械臂物体重定向任务，在抓取选择、交接、重新抓取和运动规划等多个约束下联合优化，相比基线方法成功率提升20%以上，轨迹平滑度提升37%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双机械臂物体重定向（抓取物体、双臂间传递、放置到目标位姿）在直接放置不可行时（如碰撞、运动学约束或最终朝向不佳）很有价值，但在多个竞争性目标下实现这一任务仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 提出BiCompoDiff框架，将预训练的抓取扩散模型与双臂规划基于能量的模型（EBMs）相结合，在反向扩散过程中注入梯度引导以强制执行碰撞避免、轨迹平滑性（通过可微逆运动学）、交接可行性和重新抓取安全性，并采用退火MCMC采样在组合能量景观上进一步细化抓取姿态。

Result: 在多种模拟家庭重定向任务中，BiCompoDiff相比强采样基线方法成功率提升20%以上，轨迹平滑度（以关节位移衡量）提升37%，真实世界实验验证了有效的仿真到现实迁移和鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: BiCompoDiff通过组合扩散和基于能量的框架有效解决了双臂物体重定向中的多约束联合优化问题，实现了更高的成功率和更平滑的轨迹，并具有良好的仿真到现实迁移能力。

Abstract: Bimanual object reorientation - picking an object, handing it over between two arms, and placing it in a desired target pose - is valuable when direct placement from the initial grasp is infeasible due to collisions, kinematic constraints, or poor final orientation. However, achieving this under multiple competing objectives remains challenging. We introduce BiCompoDiff, a compositional diffusion and energy-based framework that jointly optimizes grasp selection, handover, regrasp, and motion planning under multiple constraints. By combining a pretrained grasp diffusion model with bimanual planning energy-based models (EBMs), our method injects gradient guidance during reverse diffusion to enforce collision avoidance, trajectory smoothness (via differentiable inverse kinematics), handover feasibility, and regrasp safety. Annealed MCMC sampling further refines grasp poses over the composite energy landscape. Experiments across diverse simulated household reorientation tasks demonstrate that BiCompoDiff achieves over 20% higher success rates and up to 37% smoother trajectories (measured by joint displacement) compared to strong sampling-based baselines. Real-world validation confirms effective sim-to-real transfer and robust performance on challenging scenes.

</details>


### [120] [VoLN: Vision-Only Long-Horizon Navigation---Paradigm, Benchmark, and Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21400)
*Jiabin Lou,Haopeng Wang,Yuanshuai Wang,Xinyu Liu,Xuxin Lv,Yuxin Guo,Lei Huang,Rongye Shi,Wenjun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Vision-Only Long-Horizon Navigation (VoLN)，将VLN任务中由外部指令提供的路径级空间先验转移到局部可观测的场景线索中。作者在无人机导航场景下构建了VoLN-UAV基准（7,210个episode），并提出VoLN-MLLM基线方法，在测试集上Easy/Normal/Hard成功率分别为7.4%/4.5%/1.8%，揭示了长程证据融合、跨视角目标匹配和闭环稳定性等重大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN基准中，路线级指令编码了方向、距离、布局等空间先验信息，这些信息在无GPS的真实部署场景中无法通过机载传感器直接获得。因此，当前评估结果共同反映了视觉导航能力和任务描述中显式提供的路线结构信息，无法准确衡量智能体仅依赖视觉信息的真实导航能力。

Method: 提出VoLN任务公式化：目标视图指定目的地，路径相关信息仅通过局部可观测的场景线索提供。构建VoLN-UAV基准，包含7,210个episode，结合长程目标导向飞行、连续3D运动、大视角变化和上下文相关的信标选择。提出VoLN-MLLM基线方法，将自监督视觉特征与结构化语义空间对齐，从观测历史、目标视图、检索的视觉语义token和本体感知中预测短程航点段。

Result: 在5个环境的Test-Unseen分割上，Easy、Normal、Hard三种难度episode的成功率分别为7.4%、4.5%和1.8%。

Conclusion: 实验结果揭示了长程证据融合、跨视角目标匹配和闭环稳定性等重大挑战，表明VoLN任务对现有方法具有显著难度，为未来视觉导航研究提供了新的挑战性基准。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) enables embodied agents to follow natural-language instructions. However, route-level instructions commonly encode spatial priors, such as orientation, distance, and layout, that are not explicitly available from onboard sensing at deployment in open, GPS-denied environments. Benchmark performance under such interfaces therefore jointly reflects visual navigation ability and the use of route structure explicitly supplied by the task description. As a complementary formulation, we propose Vision-Only Long-Horizon Navigation (VoLN), which shifts route-relevant information from externally supplied instructions and global guidance to locally observable in-scene cues. In VoLN, goal views specify the destination, while route-relevant information is available only through locally observable in-scene cues that the agent must detect, interpret, and select online. We instantiate VoLN for aerial navigation through VoLN-UAV, a 7,210-episode benchmark that combines long-horizon goal-directed flight, continuous 3D motion, large viewpoint changes, and context-dependent beacon selection. We further provide VoLN-MLLM as an initial reference baseline. It aligns self-supervised visual features with a structured semantic space and predicts short-horizon waypoint segments from observation history, goal views, retrieved visual--semantic tokens, and proprioception. On the five-environment Test-Unseen split, it obtains success rates of 7.4%, 4.5%, and 1.8% on Easy, Normal, and Hard episodes, respectively. These results provide an initial evaluation of VoLN and reveal substantial remaining challenges in long-horizon evidence integration, cross-view goal matching, and closed-loop stability. Project page: https://admire-ljb.github.io/VoLN-UAV/

</details>


### [121] [GLAM-SLAM: Real-time Gaussian Large-scale Mapping via Flow Densification and Spatial Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21416)
*Panagiotis Mermigkas,Argyris Manetas,Petros Maragos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GLAM-SLAM 是一种面向大规模室外场景的实时、解耦式高斯泼溅 SLAM 系统。它采用基于特征的轻量跟踪前端和结构化稀疏锚点网格表示进行建图，通过极几何约束的稠密化策略和场景分区 MLP 初始化策略，在长序列数据集上实现了实时性能和 15% 的重建质量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯泼溅的单目 SLAM 系统要么针对短序列设计、无法实时运行，要么 GPU 内存需求过高，难以应用于现实中的长时程大尺度场景。

Method: 1) 解耦架构：基于特征的 SLAM 前端负责轻量跟踪，3DGS 后端负责建图；2) 结构化稀疏锚点网格表示，保证可扩展性和场景一致性；3) 基于极几何约束的几何流稠密化锚点策略，满足 3DGS 的稠密初始化需求；4) 将建图视为多场景问题，通过场景分区和 MLP 初始化引入强空间归纳偏置生成局部高斯体。

Result: 在 KITTI Odometry、Oxford RobotCar、Málaga 等长序列数据集上，重建质量相比第二名提升 15%，同时保持实时性能和对更长序列的可扩展性。

Conclusion: GLAM-SLAM 通过解耦跟踪与建图、稀疏锚点网格表示以及场景分区策略，有效解决了高斯泼溅 SLAM 在大尺度长序列场景中的实时性、内存效率和重建质量挑战。

Abstract: Existing Gaussian-splatting-based monocular Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) systems are either tailored to short sequences, are not real-time, or suffer from prohibitive GPU memory requirements, limiting their applicability in realistic, long-horizon scenarios. To address this, we present GLAM-SLAM, a real-time, decoupled Gaussian-splatting SLAM system designed for large-scale outdoor scenes. We ensure lightweight tracking using a robust, feature-based SLAM frontend, while for mapping, we adopt a structured, sparse anchor grid representation that ensures scalable operation and maintains scene coherence across long-term sequences. To satisfy the dense initialization requirements of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), we introduce a geometry-based flow-densification anchoring strategy using epipolar constraints. Furthermore, by treating mapping as a multi-scene problem, we propose a scene-partitioning strategy that introduces a strong spatial inductive bias via MLP initializations to generate localized Gaussians. We evaluate our system on the challenging, long-sequence KITTI Odometry, Oxford RobotCar, and M'alaga datasets. Extensive ablations and comparisons demonstrate a 15% improvement in reconstruction quality over the second-best performer, while maintaining real-time performance and the ability to scale to longer sequences. Code is publicly available for the benefit of the community.

</details>


### [122] [GS-Agent: Creating 4D Physical Worlds With Generative Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21522)
*Hongxin Zhang,Chunru Lin,Junyan Li,Zhou Xian,Tsun-Hsuan Wang,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GS-Agent是一个端到端多智能体框架，通过集成物理引擎从自然语言描述中生成动态、物理逼真的4D世界。它将任务分解为实体管理和渲染配置，多个专业智能体通过代码与物理引擎交互、寻求多模态反馈，协作构建符合描述的4D世界。实验证明能有效生成包含液体、可变形物体和刚体间丰富交互的多样化物理世界，并实现电影级摄像机与灯光控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统4D世界创建依赖人工手动调优材质、运动和视觉保真度，耗时耗力；现有生成方法虽然尝试从大规模数据学习，但难以保证物理合理性和可控性。作者希望借助基础模型构建一个模拟人类传统创建过程但自动化的智能系统，解决物理真实感和可控性难题。

Method: GS-Agent采用多智能体架构，将4D世界创建任务分解为两个核心模块：1) 实体管理（3D资产策划、材质调优、放置和运动控制）；2) 渲染配置（摄像机与灯光操控）。多个具备不同专业知识的智能体通过代码与物理引擎交互，接收多模态反馈，以迭代协作方式逐步构建与文本描述一致的4D世界。

Result: 实验表明GS-Agent能有效将自然语言转换为多样且物理合理的4D世界，支持液体、可变形物体和刚体之间的丰富交互，同时实现电影级摄像机与灯光控制，在物理合理性和可控性方面优于现有方法。

Conclusion: GS-Agent为4D世界生成提供了一种新范式，通过智能体系统自动化传统人工创建流程，有望赋能创意内容生成和物理AI的发展。

Abstract: Creating dynamic and physically realistic 4D worlds from natural language descriptions is both fascinating and challenging. Traditional computer graphics methods rely on manual creation, requiring extensive human effort to fine-tune materials, motions, and visual fidelity. Recent advances in generative foundation models have sparked interest in learning to generate such 4D worlds from large-scale data; however, existing methods still struggle to ensure physical plausibility and controllability. In this work, we take a different path by leveraging foundation models to construct an agentic system that emulates how humans traditionally create 4D worlds, yet automates the entire process. We present GS-Agent, an end-to-end multi-agent framework that integrates physics engines in the loop to generate realistic, dynamic, and controllable 4D physical worlds from natural language. Inspired by how humans build 4D worlds, GS-Agent decomposes the task into entity management, covering 3D asset curation, material tuning, placement, and motion control, and rendering configuration, including camera and lighting manipulation. Multiple agents with distinct expertise interact with the physics engine via code, seek multimodal feedback, and collaborate to iteratively construct 4D worlds that align with the given descriptions. Experimental results show that GS-Agent effectively converts natural language into diverse and physically plausible 4D worlds exhibiting rich interactions among liquids, deformable objects, and rigid bodies, while achieving cinematic camera and lighting control. We envision GS-Agent as a foundation for a new paradigm in 4D world generation, empowering creative content creation and physical AI. Project page at https://umass-embodied-agi.github.io/gs-agent/

</details>


### [123] [Beyond Episodic Evaluation: Memory Architectural Bottlenecks in Sequential Embodied Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21571)
*Zikui Cai,Kaushal Janga,Tan Dat Dao,Seungjae Lee,Shivin Dass,Mingyo Seo,Kaiyu Yue,Mintong Kang,Nandhu Pillai,Monte Hoover,Aadi Palnitkar,Ruchit Rawal,Ruijie Zheng,Bo Li,Yuke Zhu,Roberto Martín-Martín,Tom Goldstein,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文研究了具身问答（EQA）在连续、多查询场景下的记忆架构问题，发现传统基于占用地图或短视 episodic 记忆的方法存在信息保留不足或时序错配问题，提出结构化、空间锚定的视觉记忆（将持续视觉观测映射到度量3D几何上）能打破准确性与效率的权衡，在模拟和真实机器人实验中均取得更优效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人需要连续运行并积累、保留、选择性复用先前交互中获得的信息，但现有EQA评估采用episodic方式（每轮任务独立，内部状态重置），缺少对支持连续场景下顺序记忆的架构机制的探索。

Method: 对比不同记忆架构在顺序EQA任务中的表现：保留2D占用地图（仅记住探索轨迹但丢失视觉语义证据）、基于短视episodic数据训练（面临时序错配），并提出结构化空间锚定记忆——将持续视觉观测映射到度量3D几何上，形成连贯的场景表征。

Result: 空间锚定的视觉记忆在连续设置下打破了准确性与效率的权衡，同时实现更高的答案准确率和更低的导航成本。模拟环境和真实移动机器人实验均验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 结构化、空间锚定的视觉记忆对于实现物理环境中连续、智能的机器人操作至关重要。

Abstract: Embodied question answering (EQA) is traditionally evaluated under an episodic formulation, where agents solve each task independently and reset internal state between episodes. However, real-world robots operate continuously and must accumulate, retain, and selectively reuse information acquired from prior interactions. Despite this practical requirement, the architectural mechanisms needed to support sequential memory in EQA remain underexplored. In this work, we investigate how different memory architectures behave when EQA agents are evaluated sequentially, with multiple questions answered in the same scene while memory is carried forward across queries. We find that simply preserving existing memory is often insufficient. Agents that retain only traversability information, such as 2D occupancy maps, remember where the robot has explored but not the visual-semantic evidence needed for later questions. Agents trained on short-horizon episodic data face a different challenge: when exposed to continuous, multi-query histories, their inherited context suffers from severe temporal mismatch, rather than forming a reusable scene representation. To overcome this architectural bottleneck, we highlight the necessity of structured, spatially grounded memory: architectures that map persistent visual observations onto metric 3D geometry preserve visual-semantic evidence in a coherent scene representation. Extensive experiments in simulated environments reveal that this form of memory breaks the accuracy-efficiency tradeoff in sequential settings, simultaneously achieving higher answer accuracy and lower navigation costs. We further validate these findings on a real-world mobile robot, demonstrating that spatially grounded visual memory is critical for enabling continuous, intelligent operation in physical environments.

</details>


### [124] [Scale Up Strategically: Learning Compositional Generalization via Bias-Aware Evaluation and Data Collection for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21582)
*Yu Qi,Zhang Ye,Xinyi Xu,Yuxuan Lu,Amitoj Sandhu,Boce Hu,Haojie Huang,Jonathan Tremblay,Lawson L. S. Wong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出诊断框架量化机器人基础策略中的指令因子偏差（如颜色、动词、物体等），发现颜色主导而动词和尺寸易被忽视，并验证了基于偏差感知的数据收集策略能更高效地提升组合泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练策略在执行组合指令时倾向于利用显著线索（捷径）而非真正理解语言，导致泛化失败。现有研究缺乏对具体语义因子（如颜色、动词、物体、尺寸、空间属性）偏差的细粒度诊断和量化方法。

Method: 提出诊断框架，形式化指令因子偏差（instruction factor bias），设计两个量化指标：因子主导率（FDR）捕捉因子间的成对偏差，因子主导层级（FDH）聚合为全局排序。在六种基础策略上评估，验证偏差排序的一致性（颜色≥物体≥空间≥动词≥尺寸）。进一步提出偏差感知的数据收集策略，将固定预算重新分配给被忽视的因子。

Result: 六种基础策略上因子偏差排序一致：颜色主导，动词和尺寸最易被忽视。偏差感知的数据收集策略在仿真和真实机器人上使用一半演示样本即超越基线，实现了更高效、更泛化的策略学习。

Conclusion: 指令因子偏差是可诊断且可操作的；通过针对性数据分配可显著提升样本效率与组合泛化能力，为机器人指令跟随提供了实用改进方向。

Abstract: Compositional generalization is essential for robot to follow diverse instructions. However, pretrained policies are known to take shortcuts, deferring to salient cues rather than grounding language. We introduce a diagnostic framework that localizes this failure to individual \textit{instruction factors}, \textit{e.g.,} reusable semantic components such as color, verb, object, size, and spatial attribute. Our framework formalizes instruction factor bias, the tendency of fine-tuned policies to over-rely on dominant factors as shortcuts, and quantifies it through two metrics: Factor Dominance Rate (FDR), capturing pairwise bias between factors, and Factor Dominance Hierarchy (FDH), aggregating these into a global ranking. Evaluation on six foundation policies reveals broadly consistent ordering, \textit{i.e.}, color $\geq$ object $\geq$ spatial $\geq$ verb $\geq$ size, with color dominant, and verb and size most under-grounded. We further show the diagnosis is actionable: a bias-aware data collection strategy that reallocates a fixed budget toward under-grounded factors outperforms baselines in simulation and on a real robot using half the demonstrations, thereby enabling more sample-efficient and generalizable policy learning.

</details>


### [125] [AXIS: A Growable Community-Driven Data Engine for Scalable Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21588)
*Mengfei Zhao,Dihong Huang,Yikai Tang,Peihao Li,Mingxuan Yan,Ruiqi Zhuang,Yanjia Huang,Jie Wang,Hai Zhai,Tony Zhou,Rui Zhang,Zhexi Luo,Yuchen Huang,Jianfei Yang,Jiachen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AXIS是一个可增长的社区驱动数据引擎和基准，通过浏览器遥操作、自动任务生成验证、以及自动化数据处理流程，构建了包含207个任务和50K+轨迹的数据集。在统一评估协议下，持续预训练可使π₀.₅成功率提升5.8%，超越RoboCasa365预训练模型37.3%，并在布局、传感器噪声和相机扰动下表现出持续扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作策略数据管线因依赖专用硬件、集中化操作员或固定任务套件而难以扩展，缺乏可规模化、高质量且多样化的演示数据收集机制。

Method: 提出AXIS框架：(1) 基于浏览器的遥操作实现大规模演示收集；(2) 自动生成并验证新操作任务；(3) 自动化数据处理流程，包括成功检查、质量过滤、轨迹平滑、视觉与物理增强；(4) 构建任务快照组织和系统化的留出评估协议。

Result: AXIS数据集包含207个任务和50K+轨迹；持续预训练使π₀.₅总体成功率提升5.8%；相比RoboCasa365预训练模型提升37.3%；在布局、传感器噪声和相机扰动下表现出与数据量一致的持续扩展性。

Conclusion: AXIS证明了社区驱动、可扩展的数据引擎在机器人学习中的有效性，通过自动化流程和统一评估基准，显著提升了VLA策略的性能和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Learning effective robot manipulation policies requires diverse, high-quality demonstrations, yet existing data pipelines are often difficult to scale because they rely on specialized hardware, centralized operators, or fixed task suites. We present AXIS, a growable community-driven data engine and benchmark for scalable robot learning, which enables browser-based teleoperation for large-scale demonstration collection, automatically generates and validates new manipulation tasks, and transforms community-collected demonstrations into training-ready data through automated success checking, quality filtering, trajectory smoothing, and visual and physics-based augmentation. The AXIS dataset currently contains 207 diverse tasks and 50K+ trajectories. Meanwhile, AXIS organizes data into task snapshots and evaluates policies with a systematic held-out protocol. We compare vision-language-action (VLA) policies under a unified AXIS evaluation suite and analyze scaling behavior across different data volumes. Continual pretraining on AXIS substantially improves the overall success rate of $π_{0.5}$ by 5.8%, outperforms the model pretrained on RoboCasa365 by 37.3%, and exhibits consistent scaling with increasing data volume, with the largest gains observed under layout, sensor-noise, and camera perturbations.

</details>
